# infoCRM reference — screens of area «reports»

Source: authorized audit of the reference system on 2026-09-07 (Kurdish UI, viewport 1440×900). Screenshots: `screenshots/reference/<name>-1440.png` (viewport), `<name>-1440-full.png` (full page), `<name>-tall.png` (1440×2600), `<name>-tabN.png` (tab N), `<name>-filter.png` (filter drawer). Raw DOM summaries: `docs/reference-audit/<name>.json` (+ `<name>-filter.json`).

Icon names are Fabric MDL2 names used by the reference (`ms-Icon--Name`); render them through the shared `<Icon>` component (Fluent UI System icons).
